Prior JEPA planners often inherit that ranking from embedding geometry, typically latent Euclidean distance, which arises as a byproduct of representation learning rather than as a progress cost mined from the logs. We propose temporal-distance JEPA (TD-JEPA), which retains the LeWM encoder--predictor backbone and mines a directed temporal cost from reward-free trajectories: same-trajectory step order supplies positive targets, cross-trajectory pairs act as heuristic negatives, and a rollout-consistency term matches the planner horizon. The mined supervision serves two roles: as the deployed planning cost when progress is topological, and as a representation signal that improves Euclidean planning when contact geometry dominates. Under locked evaluation, deploying the mined cost raises Two-Room success to 100.0% versus LeWM's 97.4%, while shared Euclidean planning on the same temporally trained checkpoint raises OGB-Cube by 14.2 points over LeWM and improves Push-T. Against LeWM and the concurrent RC-aux baseline under locked evaluation, TD-JEPA matches or exceeds both methods on every environment. Ablations show that the directed head, cross-trajectory negatives, and rollout consistency each contribute. TD-JEPA narrows the train--plan gap for JEPA world-model planners by discovering temporal progress structure in offline logs and co-designing cost form with plan-time deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/HKBU-KnowComp/TD-JEPA.

</details>


### [109] [Inspect India Evals: An Open Benchmarking Framework for Evaluating Large Language Models in the Indian Linguistic and Cultural Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25375)
*Abhishek Kumar Singh,Shrey Nag,Sachita,Lipi Goel,Rajeshwar Singh Janwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 印度Inspect India Evals是一个开源的LLM评估框架，包含6个面向印度语境的基准测试（多语言MMLU、社会偏见测试、数字公共基础设施安全、多语言安全测试、多轮越狱抵抗测试、印度文化知识测试），对5个8B-32B的开源模型进行了评估，发现Sarvam-M 24B和Gemma 2 27B表现最优，综合印度公平指数达80%。所有模型在多语言安全测试中100%拒答，但数字公共基础设施安全得分差异大（20%-100%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估基准（如MMLU、BIG-Bench）几乎全是英语和西方中心化的，无法识别印度语境下特有的安全、公平和准确性问题。印度有14亿人口、22种官方语言和多样文化，但缺乏专为其设计的评估框架。

Method: 基于UK AISI的Inspect AI平台构建开源框架，设计了6个印度语境的基准测试：1）16种印度语言的多语言MMLU；2）印度社会偏见测试BharatBBQ；3）数字公共基础设施（DPI）安全评估；4）印度语言有害提示的多语言安全测试；5）多轮越狱抵抗测试；6）用LLM-as-judge打分的印度文化知识基准。测试了5个8B-32B的开源模型。

Result: Sarvam-M 24B和Gemma 2 27B综合印度公平指数达80%，表现最佳。Sarvam-M 24B在印度文化知识和DPI安全合规上甚至超过更大的32B模型。所有模型在多语言安全测试中100%拒答，但DPI安全得分从20%到100%不等。

Conclusion: Inspect India Evals框架有效填补了印度语境下LLM评估的空白，证明了在安全、公平和文化知识方面进行本土化评估的必要性。框架开源、可复现，支持UK AISI注册表，任何人都可扩展使用。

Abstract: India is a vast nation of over 1.4 billion people, varied by hundreds of diverse and locally specific traditions and cultures and 22 officially recognized languages. Large language models (LLMs) are now being deployed on a massive scale throughout the mainland as well as in remote villages. However, the common benchmarks - MMLU, BIG-Bench, and TruthfulQA are almost exclusively English- and Western-centric. They do not identify those safety, fairness, and accuracy failures unique to the Indian context. That is the gap Inspect India Evals seeks to fill. It is an open-source framework built on top of UK AISI's Inspect AI platform. It has six benchmarks: Multilingual MMLU across sixteen Indian languages, BharatBBQ (our adaptation of BBQ for Indian social bias), a safety evaluation for Digital Public Infrastructure, a multilingual safety test using harmful prompts in Indian languages, a multi-turn jailbreak resistance test, and an Indian cultural knowledge benchmark scored using LLM-as-judge rubrics. In this study, we tested five open-weight models ranging from 8B to 32B parameters. Sarvam-M 24B and Gemma 2 27B came out on top, both scoring 80% on the composite India Fairness Index, with Sarvam-M even beating larger 32B models on Indian cultural knowledge and DPI safety compliance. All models scored 100% refusal on Multilingual Safety, whereas DPI safety varied from 20% to 100%. The framework is public. It's built to work with the UK AISI registry. Anyone can reproduce or extend this work.

</details>


### [110] [Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25380)
*Sining Zhoubian,Dan Zhang,Evgeny Kharlamov,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了大语言模型中记忆机制的演化，提出了一个以架构为中心的分类法，从表示（隐式/显式）、更新动态（离线/在线）和持久性（短期/长期）三个正交轴对记忆进行刻画，并形式化了记忆写入、路由、状态转换和整合等细粒度机制，为碎片化的记忆研究提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中记忆机制发展迅速但研究高度碎片化，缺乏系统性的统一视角来理解和对比不同记忆架构之间的异同。

Method: 提出以架构为中心的三轴分类法：表示（隐式 vs 显式）、更新动态（离线 vs 在线）、持久性（短期 vs 长期），并形式化记忆写入、路由、状态转换和整合等细粒度机制。

Result: 统一了计算耦合型记忆与独立可寻址记忆之间的概念边界，批判性分析了混合记忆架构、系统级效率权衡和多维评估方法。

Conclusion: 通过将碎片化进展整合为连贯框架，为未来可扩展和自适应语言建模中的记忆中心型LLM设计提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Memory has evolved into a foundational architectural dimension in large language models (LLMs), shifting from an implicit byproduct of computation to a spectrum of explicit, controllable mechanisms. While recent advances introduce diverse strategies---spanning transient attention, recurrent state dynamics, parameter-efficient adaptations, and scalable lookup storage---this rapid evolution has led to a highly fragmented research landscape. In this survey, we present a systematic, architecture-centric taxonomy of memory in LLMs. Our framework characterizes memory along three orthogonal axes: representation (implicit versus explicit), update dynamics (offline versus online), and persistence (short-term versus long-term). We further formalize the granular mechanisms dictating memory writing, routing, state transitions, and consolidation. This unified perspective elucidates the conceptual boundaries between computation-coupled and independently addressable memory, effectively bridging disparate architectural paradigms. Additionally, we critically analyze hybrid memory architectures, system-level efficiency trade-offs, and multi-dimensional evaluation methodologies. By consolidating these scattered advancements into a cohesive framework, this survey charts the trajectory of memory-centric LLM design and provides a principled foundation for future innovations in scalable and adaptive language modeling.

</details>


### [111] [Phase Structure in Rotary Attention: A Spectral Framework for Semantic Continuity and Execution-Boundary Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25507)
*Abraham Chachamovits*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个有界谱分析框架，用于研究Transformer语言模型中RoPE旋转相位对齐、隐状态连续性和语义漂移，严格区分了表示连续性与执行边界可容许性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常通过向量几何分析语言模型，但有序上下文和旋转位置编码引入了显式的相位结构。需要一种严格的理论框架来分析相位对齐、连续性和语义漂移，同时避免将语言模型视为物理波系统。

Method: (1) 将有序列隐状态而非词汇索引作为谱分解的有效域；(2) 将RoPE注意力分数推导为幅度加权的余弦项之和；(3) 证明局部稳定性引理：有界相位位移限制预softmax分数的退化；(4) 定义复模态坐标和加权相干函数分析隐状态轨迹。

Result: 建立了表示连续性与执行边界可容许性之间的严格区分：内部相干性可以描述任务相关关系的保持，但不能授权一个结果性的转换。

Conclusion: 该框架贡献了一个理论和方法程序，用于确定谱结构何时解释连续性，以及何时治理必须保持为执行上的外部谓词。

Abstract: Transformer language models are usually analyzed through vector geometry, yet ordered context and rotary position encoding introduce explicit phase structure into query-key interactions. This paper develops a bounded spectral framework for examining rotary phase alignment, hidden-state continuity, and semantic drift without treating language models as literal physical wave systems. It first identifies ordered hidden-state sequences, rather than vocabulary indices, as valid domains for spectral decomposition. It then derives the Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) attention score as a sum of magnitude-weighted cosine terms and proves a local stability lemma: uniformly bounded phase displacement limits degradation of the corresponding pre-softmax score. To extend phase analysis beyond native RoPE coordinates, the paper defines complex modal coordinates over fixed orthonormal direction pairs and introduces a weighted coherence functional for hidden-state trajectories. These constructions support a strict distinction between representational continuity and execution-boundary admissibility. Internal coherence may describe preservation of task-relevant relations, but it cannot authorize a consequential transition. Positioned against existing geometric, spectral, phase-modulation, representation-analysis, and mechanistic-interpretability accounts, the framework contributes a theoretical and methodological program for determining when spectral structure explains continuity and when governance must remain an external predicate over execution.

</details>


### [112] [IRIS: Reusable Identity Representations from Frozen LLMs for Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25579)
*Xinran Liu,Shengtao Li,Shouqian Shi,Ge Wang,Xin-Wei Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IRIS框架，利用冻结大语言模型的内部状态为知识图谱中的每个实体构建稳定、可区分的“虹膜”式特征编码，实现无需训练、跨KG直接相似度比较的实体对齐，在四个基准上取得接近完美（Hits@1最高100.00）的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EA方法依赖显式图结构和文本字段，对异质描述下的同一实体理解不足；现有LLM-based方法仅将LLM用于辅助生成或候选条件决策，未将语义理解蒸馏为稳定、可直接比较的实体身份空间，导致对齐依赖于特定KG对或候选集，且匹配上下文变化时需重复处理。

Method: 提出IRIS框架，从冻结LLM中引出面向身份的上下文表征，为每个实体构建类似虹膜的特征签名。该签名编码实体独特且稳定的身份特征，形成共享空间，每个实体仅编码一次，通过直接相似度比较实现跨KG对齐，无需对依赖表征构建或逐候选LLM推理。

Result: 在四个EA基准上，最佳IRIS变体在D-Y-15K V2、DBP-WIKI、ICEWS-WIKI、ICEWS-YAGO上分别达到Hits@1为100.00、99.38、98.31、97.99。

Conclusion: IRIS通过从LLM内部状态提取稳定身份特征，实现了训练无关、跨KG直接比较的实体对齐，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Entity alignment (EA) identifies entities across knowledge graphs (KGs) that refer to the same real-world object. Conventional EA methods mainly exploit explicit graph structures and textual fields, which often provide insufficient semantic understanding to recognize the same entity under heterogeneous descriptions and distinguish it from semantically similar entities. Although large language models (LLMs) offer deeper entity understanding, existing LLM-based EA methods largely use this capability for auxiliary generation or candidate-conditioned decisions. Consequently, such understanding is not distilled into a stable and directly comparable identity space, leaving alignment tied to specific KG pairs or candidate sets and requiring repeated processing as the matching context changes. To address these limitations, we propose IRIS (Identity Representations from Internal States), a training-free framework that constructs for each entity an iris-like signature encoding its distinctive and stable identity characteristics. IRIS derives these signatures by eliciting identity-oriented contextual representations from a frozen LLM, thereby forming a shared space in which each entity is encoded once and can be aligned across different KGs through direct similarity comparison, without pair-dependent representation construction or candidate-wise LLM inference. Across four established EA benchmarks and two frozen LLM backbones, the best IRIS variants achieve Hits@1 scores of 100.00, 99.38, 98.31, and 97.99 on D-Y-15K V2, DBP-WIKI, ICEWS-WIKI, and ICEWS-YAGO, respectively.

</details>


### [113] [Evaluation of forced alignment of code-mixed speech: the case of Hindi-English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25581)
*Ayushi Pandey,Pamir Gogoi,Kevin Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在印地语-英语混合语音中进行强制对齐的挑战，并提出通过词典设计和混合训练数据来显著提升对齐精度，平均误差低至4.15ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合语音中存在音素库存扩大、拼写错误和说话人变异等问题，传统强制对齐方法效果不佳，需要针对性的建模策略。

Method: 使用Montreal Forced Aligner，针对自由变异（本地vs非本地音素对）和语中英语词汇的音素边界检测两个问题，采用引导策略（bootstrapping）和基于句子级混合语音数据训练的声学模型。

Result: 混合语音训练的声学模型平均误差仅4.15ms，远优于单语印地语（38.18ms）或孤立英语（37.58ms）模型。

Conclusion: 合理的词典设计与混合语音训练数据对实现可靠的双语语音对齐至关重要。

Abstract: Code-mixed speech poses unique challenges to forced alignment: expanded inventories, orthographic errors, and speaker variation. We evaluate forced alignment of Hindi-English code-mixed speech using the Montreal Forced Aligner. We address 2 problems: (1) free variation involving native vs non-native pairs and (2) phonemic boundary detection for mid-utterance English words. Bootstrapping strategies substantially outperform unmodified lexicons. Acoustic models trained on sentence-level code-mixed data achieve a mean error of 4.15ms, ie. ten times lower than monolingual Hindi (38.18ms) or isolated English (37.58ms) alternatives. Principled lexicon design and code-mixed training data are both essential for reliable alignment of bilingual speech.

</details>


### [114] [PILA: Plug-and-Play Insertion for LLM-native Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25590)
*Zhaowei Zhang,Yuhan Fu,Yihang Zhang,Xiaohan Liu,Ceyao Zhang,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yipeng Kang,Tonghan Wang,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PILA提出了一种轻量级侧车模块，将广告插入解耦为条件响应重写问题，可与现有LLM服务无缝集成，在不影响原始回复质量的前提下实现原生广告投放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM原生广告方案将广告与内容生成耦合在同一模型中，无法兼容现代API-only或工作流式LLM应用，且会不可避免损害原始回复质量。

Method: 将广告插入重新定义为条件响应重写问题，设计为与上游服务解耦的轻量级侧车模块（sidecar），支持模型无关、可控的用户自然度与广告曝光度权衡。

Result: 在多种上游模型上的实验表明，PILA能够持续提升广告效果，同时保持回复质量。

Conclusion: PILA是一种实用、解耦且可控的LLM原生广告解决方案，具有良好的部署前景。

Abstract: How to monetize large language models (LLMs) by naturally integrating sponsored content into their responses, known as LLM-native advertising, has recently emerged as a critical problem. However, existing solutions entangle advertising with content generation inside a single model, which is incompatible with modern API-only or workflow-based LLM applications and inevitably compromises the original response quality. To address this, we propose PILA, which reformulates ad insertion as a conditional response rewriting problem and decouples it from the upstream service as a lightweight sidecar module. PILA is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated with existing LLM services without modifying the base model or its workflow. It also exposes a controllable trade-off between user-side naturalness and ad-side exposure, offering a practical interface for downstream pricing and deployment. Experiments across diverse upstream models show that \pila consistently improves ad effectiveness while preserving response quality, highlighting its promise as a practical solution for LLM-native advertising.

</details>


### [115] [A Human-in-the-Loop Corpus for LLM-Based Simplification of Scientific Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25630)
*Kyuri Im,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合人类反馈的LLM科学文本简化流程，构建了跨学科简化语料库，实验表明GPT生成的简化在可理解性和简洁性上更受青睐，而专家编辑则强调了保留专业术语和科学主张强度的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科研究日益重要，但科学论文对非本领域研究者难以理解，现有简化方法缺乏针对非专业读者的定制化流程和评估资源。

Method: 以SciSummNet为源语料，先用GPT-4o-mini生成基线简化；Phase 1由非CS领域的STEM读者评估原始与GPT简化版本的可理解性、自然性和简洁性；Phase 2由CS专家根据读者反馈创建专家编辑参考简化。最终释放包含人工评判和自动评估的语料库。

Result: Phase 1中读者明显偏好GPT生成的简化版本；Phase 2的定性分析显示专家编辑注重保留领域术语和科学主张的强度。

Conclusion: 所构建的语料库和人工评判数据可用于训练和基准测试面向跨学科科学交流的简化系统。

Abstract: Interdisciplinary research is accelerating, yet scientific papers remain difficult to understand outside their home fields. We study large language model (LLM)-based simplification of scientific texts and present a human-in-the-loop workflow that transforms expert summaries into more accessible versions for non-specialists. Using SciSummNet as the source corpus, we first generate baseline simplifications with GPT-4o-mini. In Phase 1, readers from STEM fields outside computer science identify difficult sentences and phrases and compare the original and GPT-simplified summaries in terms of comprehensibility, naturalness, and simplicity. In Phase 2, computer science experts use this feedback to create expert-edited reference simplifications. We release the resulting corpus together with human judgments and automatic evaluation results. The Phase 1 judgments show a clear preference for the GPT-generated summaries in terms of comprehensibility and simplicity, while qualitative analysis of the Phase 2 edits highlights the importance of preserving domain-specific terminology and the strength of scientific claims. The resulting resource supports the training and benchmarking of simplification systems for cross-disciplinary scientific communication.

</details>


### [116] [Construction-Driven Injection: Linguistically-Grounded Edit-Based Code-Mixing Fingerprints for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25633)
*Yongyi Cui,Yue Li,Tianbao Jiang,Xin Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LCF框架，通过联合优化指纹构造与注入，利用低资源语言混合生成代码混合指纹，并借助多语言零空间投影实现知识保留的注入，解决了现有方法容易误激活或被困惑度检测过滤的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指纹构造与注入两个阶段相互解耦，导致自然语言指纹易误激活、乱码指纹易被困惑度检测过滤，且注入阶段无法感知触发词的 linguistic 结构，错过针对性优化机会。

Method: LCF框架：1) LCF构造代码混合指纹（低资源语言+语义密度替换+语法偏置混合）；2) LCFEdit利用高资源多语言表示的零空间投影注入，并结合跨语言对齐更新。

Result: 在不显著影响模型能力的前提下，实现了持久的版权验证，并具有良好的不易察觉性、可检测性和无害性。

Conclusion: 指纹构造应驱动注入过程，联合优化两个阶段能产生更稳定、更隐蔽的版权保护信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are costly intellectual assets that remain exposed to unauthorized redistribution and commercial misuse. Injected fingerprints, i.e., trigger--target pairs embedded in model behavior, offer a practical, black-box-verifiable ownership signal, but existing methods decouple the two stages of the fingerprint life cycle: how a fingerprint is constructed and how it is injected. Existing fingerprinting frameworks suffer from two limitations. Natural-language fingerprints are prone to accidental activation, and garbled fingerprints are easily filtered by perplexity-based detection. Furthermore, decoupling construction from injection leaves the latter unaware of the trigger's linguistic structure, missing the opportunity for targeted optimization. We argue that fingerprint construction should drive injection, and present a unified fingerprinting framework that jointly optimizes both stages. First, LCF constructs code-mixing fingerprints by combining low-resource languages under a semantic-density substitution rule and grammar-biased mixing, yielding triggers whose perplexity sits far below garbled baselines while avoiding the accidental-activation failures of natural-language triggers. Second, LCFEdit injects each fingerprint with a null-space projection derived from high-resource multilingual representations that preserves knowledge, augmented by a cross-lingual alignment step that steers the weight update toward the fingerprint language's representation subspace. This construction-aware injection ensures that the update is linguistically informed and therefore more stable. Extensive evaluations on imperceptibility, detectability, and harmlessness demonstrate persistent ownership verification with negligible impact on utility.

</details>


### [117] [MyMentorLLM: A psychotherapy GenAI environment with multimodal voice/text patients, trainees and experts for deliberate practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25667)
*Rodolfo Rizzi,Alessandro Grecucci,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MyMentorLLM，一个用于心理治疗师刻意练习的多模态语音与文本模拟环境，生成了2,100次完整的CBT训练会话，模拟了患者、受训治疗师和专家督导之间的互动，验证了AI模拟在治疗训练中的可行性与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理治疗师需要反复的专家培训和督导，但可扩展性是个问题。传统方式成本高、资源有限，难以大规模推广，因此需要借助AI模拟环境来实现规模化训练。

Method: 构建了MyMentorLLM多模态模拟环境，基于DSM-5-TR生成三类障碍患者（重度抑郁、广泛性焦虑、边缘型人格），让AI模拟受训治疗师与患者互动，并由AI专家督导提供反馈。生成了2,100次完整的CBT训练会话。

Result: 模拟患者表现出与疾病一致的情绪特征，受训治疗师也如真实咨询中一样产生了情绪镜像。大多数LLM高估了受训者的能力，但原生语音到语音模型最接近人类评分。督导反馈在5/7的LLM中改善了诊断准确性，症状识别能力随模型规模增大而提高。

Conclusion: 研究证明了CBT训练中刻意练习模拟的可行性，但患者保真度、督导校准和有害反馈问题仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Psychotherapists need repeated training and supervision by experts; however, scalability is problematic. Here we present MyMentorLLM, a multimodal voice- and text-based simulation environment for deliberate practice, used to generate 2,100 complete Cognitive Behavioural Therapy (CBT) training sessions. Each session links a DSM-5-TR-grounded patient (with major depressive, generalised anxiety or borderline personality disorder), a therapist-in-training and an expert supervisor. As an initial implementation, we adopted CBT because its structured procedures and competency-based supervision facilitate standardised simulation and evaluation. Sessions were analysed for emotional dynamics, therapeutic competence and diagnostic accuracy. Simulated patients expressed disorder-congruent emotional profiles, which trainee therapists mirrored as in real human counselling. The quality of supervision differed across LLMs: while most models overestimated trainees' competences, native speech-to-speech was closest to human scores. Supervisors' feedback led to better diagnoses in simulated psychotherapists in 5 out of 7 LLMs, and symptom identification accuracy increased with model size. This work shows that simulation of deliberate practice is possible for CBT training, although patient fidelity, calibration of supervisors, and harmful feedback should be evaluated together.

</details>


### [118] [SpeechLLM Meets Federated Learning for End-to-End ASR: English and Italian Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25716)
*Mohamed Nabih Ali,Daniele Falavigna,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了联邦学习在语音大模型(SpeechLLM)端到端ASR系统中的应用，提出了一种通信高效的联邦优化策略，在英语和意大利语单语ASR任务上验证了其有效性和稳定性，实现了有竞争力的词错误率并降低了通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽已用于ASR系统保护隐私训练，但针对大规模语音语言模型(SpeechLLMs)的联邦训练尚未被探索，存在高维参数空间、梯度通信开销和分布式计算约束等独特挑战。

Method: 设计了一种通信高效的联邦优化策略：①针对SpeechLLM架构的高维参数空间优化通信；②减少梯度通信开销；③适应分布式计算约束；④在英语和意大利语的不同声学条件和说话风格下进行实验；⑤对多种语音编码器架构进行消融研究。

Result: 在英语和意大利语单语ASR任务上，联邦方法相比集中式训练基线取得了有竞争力的词错误率(WER)，同时显著降低了通信成本；消融研究揭示了不同语音编码器架构在联邦框架下的最优配置。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了联邦SpeechLLM在真实世界多语言场景中部署的实践基础，证明了联邦训练SpeechLLM的可行性、有效性和通信效率优势。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving training of automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems across distributed data sources, yet its application to large-scale speech language models (SpeechLLMs) remains unexplored. This paper presents the first systematic study of federated training for SpeechLLM-based end-to-end ASR systems. We design a communication-efficient federated optimization strategy tailored to the unique challenges of SpeechLLM architectures, addressing high-dimensional parameter spaces, gradient communication overhead, and computational constraints in distributed settings. Through extensive empirical evaluation on monolingual ASR tasks in English and Italian, we demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of our federated approach compared to centralized training baselines across diverse acoustic conditions and speaking styles. Additionally, we conduct a comprehensive ablation study analyzing the impact of different speech encoder architectures on monolingual English ASR performance within the federated framework, providing insights into optimal model configurations for decentralized training. Our results achieve competitive word error rates while reducing communication costs, establishing practical foundations for federated SpeechLLM deployment in real-world multilingual scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [WorkSurface-Bench: Benchmarking Enterprise Agents on Multi-Surface Knowledge Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25765)
*Hao Liang,Meiyi Qiang,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WorkSurface-Bench 是一个包含1,151个原子任务的基准测试，用于评估企业智能体在文档、表格、图结构和跨表面问题中正确选择知识源（表面路由）的能力。实验表明，即使表面选择正确（Route F1达98.7-99.8%），答案准确率仍仅56.1-75.3%，说明正确路由必要但不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常分别评估检索或工具使用能力，但忽略了智能体是否首先选择了正确的知识源这一关键步骤。企业智能体需要整合异构知识源（文档、表格、依赖图），因此需要专门评估其表面路由能力。

Method: 构建了1,151个原子任务，源自persona-scoped Workspace-Bench-Lite工作空间，涵盖文档、表格、图和跨表面问题。答案可审计：表格答案通过DuckDB查询复现，文档答案基于验证的文本跨度，图答案追踪到源依赖注释。在4个模型骨干和6种受控智能体设置下评估，获得27,624条无协议错误轨迹。

Result: 在黄金约束工具访问下，智能体Route F1达98.7-99.8%，但Answer仅56.1-75.3%。表面提示改善三个模型的Answer，移除无关工具主要提升路由和效率。200个任务的三标注者审计中，所有任务通过全部六项质量标准（多数投票），192个任务获得一致评判。

Conclusion: 正确的表面选择是任务完成的必要条件但不充分条件；表面路由能力需要独立评估；数据集和工具已开源。

Abstract: Enterprise agents often need to integrate heterogeneous knowledge sources: documents for narrative facts, tables for computation, and dependency graphs for file relationships. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate retrieval or tool use without distinguishing whether an agent first selects the appropriate knowledge sources. We introduce WorkSurface-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating this capability as surface routing. It contains 1,151 atomic tasks derived from persona-scoped Workspace-Bench-Lite workspaces, spanning document, table, graph, and cross-surface questions. Its reference answers are auditable: table answers are reproduced through executed DuckDB queries, document answers are grounded in verified text spans, and graph answers are traced to source dependency annotations. We evaluate four model backbones across six controlled agent settings, yielding 27,624 protocol-error-free trajectories. Under gold-constrained tool access, agents achieve 98.7-99.8 Route F1, while Answer remains only 56.1-75.3 percent, showing that correct surface selection is necessary but insufficient for task completion. Matched interventions further show that surface hints improve Answer for three of four models, whereas removing irrelevant tools primarily improves routing and efficiency. In an independent three-annotator audit, all 200 sampled tasks pass all six quality criteria by majority vote, with 192 receiving unanimous judgments on every criterion. We release the dataset, construction pipeline, scoring code, and agent harness at https://github.com/haolpku/WorkSurface-Bench.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluation of Adversarial Robustness in Arabic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25814)
*Anwar Alajmi,Ayed Salman,Imtiaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了五种先进阿拉伯语语言模型在不同粒度（字符、词、句子）对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，发现插入变音符号可降低模型准确率高达92%，词级联词操作导致58%性能下降，句子级改写平均降低76%性能；对抗训练虽能提升整体韧性，但对字符级噪声仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着阿拉伯语语言模型能力显著提升，其安全漏洞也随之暴露，特别是对抗攻击可欺骗模型做出错误预测，引发严重的安全与可靠性担忧。目前缺乏针对阿拉伯语这类形态丰富语言的系统性鲁棒性评估。

Method: 对五种SOTA阿拉伯语模型（包括MARBERT、AraBERT等）施加多粒度（字符级、词级、句子级）对抗攻击，采用不同扰动生成策略；并探索基于对抗训练的防御技术。

Result: 字符级：插入变音符号使部分模型准确率下降92%，且扰动距离小；词级：操纵连接词保留高语义相似度，准确率降低58%；句子级：改写在平均降低76%性能；对抗训练中MARBERT最鲁棒，AraBERT提升幅度最大，但对字符级噪声防御不足。

Conclusion: 当前防御策略在形态丰富语言（如阿拉伯语）中展现潜力但存在局限，尤其对字符级噪声防御不足，需进一步研究更鲁棒的防御机制。

Abstract: The emergence of the recent outstanding capabilities of Arabic Language Models has opened doors for exposing their vulnerabilities. One of the major security risks associated with such Natural Language Processing models is adversarial attacks. These attacks can deceive the model into the wrong prediction, raising critical model security and safety concerns. This study aims to assess the robustness of five state-of-the-art Arabic Language Models under a distinct set of Arabic adversarial attacks applied at various levels of granularity and using different example generation strategies. We also explore a defense technique based on adversarial training to enhance model robustness. The results show that insertion of diacritics can reduce the accuracy of some models by 92% while maintaining a low perturbation distance. For word-level attacks, manipulating Arabic conjunctions preserves high semantic similarity scores, low perturbation distance, and leads to an accuracy degradation of up to 58%. For sentence-level attacks, paraphrasing proves its effectiveness by an average reduction of 76% in the victim models' performance. While adversarial training improves overall resilience, with MARBERT being the most robust and AraBERT showing the greatest relative gains, challenges persist, particularly against character-level noise. These findings highlight both the potential and limitations of current defense strategies in morphologically rich languages like Arabic.

</details>


### [121] [AngelSpec: Towards Real-World High Performance Inference with Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25852)
*Hong Liu,Rui Cen,Junhan Shi,Guangshuo Qin,Jiebin Zhang,Tianyu Liu,Runzhi Fan,Guoliang Zhao,Ruobing Xie,Kai Zhang,Song Liu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AngelSpec通过训练层面（MTP用于对话、块扩散用于代码数学）、架构层面（DFly：混合目标条件骨干+前驱条件自回归头）和推理层面（自适应验证深度调整）三个层次的协同优化，统一了MTP和块并行推测解码，在Hy3系列上实现1.98-2.40x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码中，自回归多token预测（MTP）和块并行扩散在不同工作负载下表现各异，没有一种草案结构能普遍最优，亟需一个统一框架来适配输出分布的异构性。

Method: 训练层：MTP草案器用对话数据训练，块扩散草案器用代码/数学数据训练；架构层：提出DFly，融合混合目标条件骨干和前驱条件自回归头；推理层：将验证视为共享批级资源，动态分配计算到高置信度前缀，结合期望效用和成本模型在线调整验证深度。

Result: 在Hy3系列上，DFly在Hy3-A21B上将平均接受长度提升约30%；在并发数4-64范围内，相较自回归解码实现1.98-2.40x加速，吞吐量比DFlash高10.5-11.8%。

Conclusion: AngelSpec证明了针对不同数据分布定制化训练草案结构、结合高效架构设计与自适应推理策略，能够有效统一MTP和块并行推测解码，显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference without changing the target distribution, but no single drafting structure performs best across real-world workloads. Autoregressive multi-token prediction (MTP) is a lightweight, stable proposal mechanism, whereas block-parallel diffusion amortizes drafting latency over much longer candidate sequences; the better choice depends strongly on the output distribution. We present AngelSpec, a unified training framework for MTP and block-parallel speculative decoding that addresses this heterogeneity at three levels. At the training level, rather than fitting one universal drafter to a uniform data mixture, we co-specialize structure and data: the MTP drafter is trained on diverse conversational data for high-entropy open-ended chat, and the block-diffusion drafter on code and mathematics data for longer predictable continuations. At the architecture level, we propose DFly, a block-diffusion framework combining a hybrid target-conditioning backbone with a predecessor-conditioned autoregressive head, improving target-feature utilization and intra-block dependency modeling while keeping generation parallel. At the inference level, both acceptance length and verification cost vary with domain, request, online load, and hardware, so DFly treats verification as a shared batch-level resource: it reallocates compute toward high-confidence prefixes across requests and combines expected utility with a profiled cost model to adapt verification depth online. Across the Hy3 series, DFly raises the average accepted length on Hy3-A21B by roughly 30% and attains the highest average throughput at every tested concurrency from 4 to 64, a 1.98-2.40x speedup over autoregressive decoding and 10.5-11.8% higher throughput than DFlash. We release AngelSpec to support training and extending these methods.

</details>


### [122] [Shieldstral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25857)
*Antonia Calvi,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Giada Pistilli,Guillaume Lample,Maarten Buyl,Maximilian Augustin,Maximilian Müller,Pierre Stock,Tom Bewley,Wassim Bouaziz,Yimu Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Shieldstral是一个3B参数的多模态安全分类器，通过将内容审核统一为二元问答任务，在文本安全基准上匹配或超越近7倍大的模型，并开创了多模态安全分类的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分类器要么规模过大难以部署，要么无法适应不同政策需求，且缺乏统一的多模态安全分类框架。

Method: 将内容审核转化为二元问答任务，统一异构安全数据集；构建约5410万样本的数据集和细粒度评估集，采用策略自适应训练方案。

Result: 3B参数的模型匹配或超越近7倍大的模型，在多模态安全分类上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过统一框架和小型自适应模型，可以在显著降低模型规模的同时保持甚至超越大型模型的性能，验证了数据质量和方法设计的重要性。

Abstract: We introduce Shieldstral, a 3B-parameter policy-adaptive multimodal safety classifier that matches or outperforms models nearly 7$\times$ its size on text safety benchmarks and sets a new state of the art on multimodal safety classification. Shieldstral formulates content moderation as a binary question-answering task. This simple formulation unifies diverse moderation tasks into a single yes/no problem, enabling heterogeneous safety datasets with divergent taxonomies to be consolidated under one training framework. We present the data construction recipe, covering curation and generation of approximately 54.1M samples and a fine-grained evaluation set to evaluate policy adaptability. Together, these enable a small adaptive model to match or outperform much larger models.

</details>


### [123] [AI's Capability in Assisting Scientific Research in Physics, Astrophysics, and Cosmology II: Project Planning and Proposal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25881)
*Jia Liu,Veena Krishnaraj,Kateryna Vovk,Kosuke Aizawa,Adrian E. Bayer,Linda Blot,Jessica Cowell,Suyog Garg,Jonathan Grée,Anamaria Hell,Ben Horowitz,Masaya Ichikawa,Kanyuni Iemoto,Keigo Kondo,Zacharie Lorsin,Kevin McCarthy,Jamie Robinson,Miguel Ruiz-Granda,Leander Thiele,Ievgen Vovk,Mingshen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了LLM能否协助科学项目规划与提案评估。人类与三种LLM（ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek）为8个专家构思的研究项目各撰写一份提案，由4名人类评审和2个前沿LLM进行盲评。结果显示人类评审对AI与人类提案评分相似，但AI评审偏向AI提案（高出约1分）。人类识别提案来源准确率72-79%，而AI评审达到100%。结论是LLM能生成可与人类媲美的提案，但在评估中需谨慎使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在科学项目规划与提案评估中的应用潜力与风险，特别是AI生成内容与人类创作的差异，以及AI评审是否存在系统偏见。

Method: 选取8个专家构思的研究项目，由人类和三种LLM（ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek）各生成一份项目计划（共32份），由4名人类评审和2个AI评审（Claude Opus 4.8、ChatGPT Pro 5.5）按照四维度标准进行盲评，同时要求评审判断提案来源（人类或AI）。

Result: 人类评审对AI与人类提案评分整体相似；AI评审对AI提案评分比人类提案高约1分（5分制）。人类识别提案来源准确率分别为72%（识别人类）和79%（识别AI），而AI评审准确率均达100%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM能生成与人类水平相当的项目计划，但AI评审系统性地偏好AI生成提案，提示在提案准备与评估中广泛部署LLM需持谨慎态度。

Abstract: We investigate how well large language models (LLMs) can assist scientific project planning and proposal evaluation. One-page project plans were independently generated for eight expert-conceived research projects in physics, astrophysics, and cosmology by human researchers and three contemporary LLMs (ChatGPT, Claude, and DeepSeek; mid-2025 models, used with their default tool access). The resulting 32 proposals were blindly evaluated by four human reviewers and two newer frontier LLMs (Claude Opus 4.8 and ChatGPT Pro 5.5) using a four-aspect evaluation rubric. Reviewers were also asked to identify whether each proposal was written by a human or an AI. Human reviewers rated human- and AI-written proposals similarly overall, whereas both AI reviewers scored AI-written proposals about one point higher (on a five-point scale) than human-written proposals. Human reviewers correctly identified human- and AI-written proposals 72% and 79% of the time, respectively, while both AI reviewers correctly classified all 32 proposals (100%). These results suggest that current LLMs can produce project plans comparable to human-written ones in the eyes of human reviewers, but that AI reviewers show a systematic preference for AI-generated proposals. Our results suggest caution when deploying LLMs widely in proposal preparation and evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Evaluating Multi-Turn Multimodal Diagnostic Reasoning on Challenging Real-World Clinical Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25933)
*Rui Yang,Weihao Xuan,Yi Lin,Zhuhan Bao,Jonathan Chong Kai Liew,Matthew Yu Heng Wong,Nicolás Lescano,Nikita R. Paripati,Emily Ling-Lin Pai,Jiarui Liu,Heli Qi,Heng-Jui Chang,Benny Kai Guo Loo,Huitao Li,Kunyu Yu,Yufan Wang,Chuan Hong,Shijian Lu,Douglas Teodoro,Naoto Yokoya,Ross Koppel,Mona Diab,Hua Xu,David W. Bates,Nan Liu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ClinMM-Bench，一个包含1,089个真实临床病例和3,760张医学图像的大规模多轮多模态临床诊断评估基准，系统性评估了15个MLLM的诊断准确性和推理质量，发现现有模型在完全正确诊断和可靠推理方面仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估多基于单轮或孤立任务，无法反映临床实践中多模态信息逐步呈现、诊断假设动态更新和临床推理持续精炼的真实复杂性，因此亟需一个更贴近临床实际的多轮多模态诊断评估基准。

Method: 构建了ClinMM-Bench基准，包含8个专科的1,089个真实病例和3,760张医学图像；采用双层评估框架（诊断准确性和诊断推理质量）；系统评估了15个代表性MLLM，并进行错误分析归纳失败模式。

Result: 闭源模型在总体诊断准确率上最高，但所有模型的完全正确诊断比例均有限；模型能识别合理的诊断方向，但在生成可靠诊断推理方面存在明显不足；错误分析识别出五种典型失败模式：信息整合失败、知识映射错误、感知错误、过早定论和视觉幻觉。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在模拟真实临床诊断过程方面仍有较大差距，尤其在诊断推理的可靠性上存在显著局限，ClinMM-Bench为未来模型改进提供了有价值的评估框架和错误分析参考。

Abstract: Clinical diagnostic evaluation should not only assess whether models can provide correct diagnoses, but also reflect the realities of clinical practice, including progressive disclosure of multimodal information, dynamic updating of diagnostic hypotheses, and continuous refinement of clinical reasoning. However, existing evaluations of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically rely on single-turn or isolated tasks, making it difficult to fully capture the complexity of real-world clinical diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we developed ClinMM-Bench, the largest multi-turn multimodal clinical diagnostic evaluation benchmark to date. ClinMM-Bench contains 1,089 challenging real-world clinical cases and 3,760 medical images across eight specialties. We systematically evaluated 15 representative MLLMs using a two-level evaluation framework that assessed both diagnostic accuracy and diagnostic reasoning quality. Results showed that proprietary models achieved the highest overall diagnostic accuracy, but the proportion of completely correct diagnoses remained limited across all models. In terms of diagnostic reasoning quality, current models can identify plausible diagnostic directions but still have considerable limitations in generating reliable diagnostic reasoning. Error analysis further identified five representative failure modes: information synthesis failure, knowledge mapping error, perception error, premature closure, and visual hallucination.

</details>


### [125] [Polistemics: Evaluating LLMs as Information Mediators in Politics & Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25953)
*Baran Peters*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Polistemics基准，用于评估大语言模型在选举中调解政治信息的能力。通过对2025年德国和荷兰选举的测试，发现高分模型在信息清晰时表现良好，但在信息缺失、模糊或矛盾时系统性失败，且存在党派偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏标准化方法来评估LLMs是否负责任地调解公民所依赖的政治信息，现有研究将这一任务视为"再现"而非"调解"，忽视了认知维度及与不完美信息的交互问题。

Method: 提出基于"认知谦逊"规范性标准的Polistemics基准，在可控设置中变化信息的清晰度、噪声和一致性等属性，对三个最先进的LLM在2025年德国和荷兰选举上进行评估。

Result: 高聚合分数掩盖了系统性失败：模型在清晰信息下调解可靠，但在信息缺失、模糊或矛盾时崩溃；同时会削弱政治语言的强度；失败由党派先验驱动，受党派标签和输出语言影响。

Conclusion: 可靠的政治信息调解是可以实现的，但目前没有模型能一致地做到这一点。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly mediate the political information citizens rely on, there is still no standardized way to assess whether they do so responsibly. We introduce Polistemics, a theory-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLMs as mediators of political information in elections. Prior work has treated this task as reproduction rather than mediation, leaving its epistemic dimensions and interaction with imperfect information unaddressed. We ground the evaluation in Epistemic Modesty, a normative standard derived from citizens' epistemic agency, and test it across controlled settings that vary informational properties such as clarity, noise, and consistency. Applying the benchmark to three state-of-the-art LLMs on the 2025 German and Dutch elections, we find that high aggregate scores mask systematic failures. Models mediate reliably under clear evidence but break down under absent, vague, or contradictory information, while flattening the intensity of political language. These failures are likely driven by party priors, influenced by party labels and output language. Reliable mediation appears achievable, but no model delivers it consistently.

</details>


### [126] [Detecting Knowledge Inconsistencies Across Text, Tables, and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25959)
*Fanfu Wei,Thibault Ehrhart,Raphaël Troncy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究Wikipedia和Wikidata中文本、表格与知识图谱三种知识模态之间的不一致性检测问题，提出分类体系与Kontrast自动框架，通过Text-to-SPARQL和LLM推理对比表格答案与知识图谱证据，实验证明跨模态不一致现象普遍存在且具有信息价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wikipedia与Wikidata的知识在多模态间深度关联却分散分布，当文本、表格和知识图谱等模态产生分歧时，如何检测并解释冲突是一个实际且未被充分研究的问题。

Method: 提出Kontrast框架，利用Text-to-SPARQL将表格查询转为知识图谱查询，结合LLM推理对比表格答案与KG证据，并按粒度差异、直接冲突、时间变化和KG不完整性等类别对不一致进行分类。

Result: 在多种Table-QA数据集上实验表明跨模态不一致现象普遍存在，不仅能发现真正的知识冲突，还能揭示缺失的KG结构、时间错配等有价值的信息，但受限于Text-to-SPARQL错误和噪声。

Conclusion: 文本、表格和知识图谱可通过系统比较相互补充与纠正，Kontrast为大规模知识审计提供了实用工具，并为未来跨模态知识一致性研究建立了基准。

Abstract: Wikipedia and Wikidata are widely used for information access, LLM pre-training, and retrieval-augmented generation. Their knowledge is deeply connected but scattered across text, tables, and knowledge graphs. This raises a practical question: when these modalities disagree, how can we detect and explain the conflict? We study this problem as \emph{modality-level inconsistency detection}. We first introduce a taxonomy of cross-modal knowledge inconsistencies, covering information granularity differences, direct conflicts, temporal changes, and KG incompleteness. We then present \textsc{Kontrast}, an automatic framework that uses Text-to-SPARQL and LLM reasoning to compare table-based answers with KG evidence and categorize the resulting inconsistencies. Experiments on various Table-QA datasets show that cross-modal inconsistencies are common and informative. They reveal not only true knowledge conflicts, but also missing KG structure and temporal mismatches while being limited by Text-to-SPARQL errors and noise. Our analysis shows that text, tables, and KGs can complement and correct one another through systematic comparison. \textsc{Kontrast} provides a practical tool for large-scale knowledge auditing and establishes a benchmark for future work on cross-modal knowledge consistency. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ECLADATTA/KONTRAST.

</details>


### [127] [Instruction-Tuned Models Locally Reuse Human Syntax More Than Humans Do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26015)
*Zandi Eberstadt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探究大型语言模型（LLMs）在对话中是否表现出与人类类似的句法趋同现象。使用替代范式数据，分析了16个Llama和Gemma模型（1B-70B），发现所有模型都表现出CFG规则复用，且指令微调增强了这一现象，但同时也增加了对无关刺激的伪趋同，且条件规则复用概率低于人类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句法趋同是人类对话中普遍存在且通常无意识的语言适应现象，但大型语言模型是否在与人类用户对话时表现出类似的句法趋同，以及这种趋同是否跨越多种句法结构，尚不清楚。

Method: 采用替代范式数据（模型生成替换人类对话中的话轮），测量相邻话轮间上下文无关文法（CFG）规则的复用率。分析了16个开源Llama和Gemma模型（1B-70B，包括预训练和指令微调版本），每个模型在1,901个匹配位置进行测试。

Result: 所有模型对前序人类话轮的CFG规则复用率均显著高于随机基线；低频规则的差异更大。指令微调模型的实际自然输出复用率高于人类基线，且指令微调后所有模型的实际复用率均提升。但指令微调模型对无关刺激的复用率也更高，实际-随机增量更小，条件规则复用几率更低（控制目标规则集大小后）。探索性分析显示，所有模型的词汇和语义相似度均高于人类基线。

Conclusion: LLMs表现出与人类类似的句法趋同现象，指令微调增强了表面上的趋同行为，但也引入了更多的伪趋同（对无关刺激的过度适应）。模型在趋同的精确性和特异性上仍与人类存在差异。

Abstract: Syntactic convergence (the tendency of speakers to adapt in language towards the grammatical profiles of their interlocutors) is a well-documented feature of human dialogue widely considered to operate below conscious awareness. Whether large language models exhibit analogous syntactic convergence toward human users relative to human baselines and across a broad range of syntactic constructions remains an open question. Using substitution-paradigm data in which model generations replace one speaker's turns in pre-existing human dialogues, this study measures turn-adjacent reuse of context-free grammar (CFG) rules across sixteen open-weight Llama and Gemma models (1B-70B, pretrained and instruction-tuned) at 1,901 matched positions per model. Every model showed greater CFG-rule overlap with the preceding human turn than with a sampled unrelated human prime, and in every model this actual-versus-random difference was larger for lower-frequency rules. Each instruction-tuned model also showed greater natural-output overlap with the actual prime than the human response it replaced, and all eight matched architecture pairs exhibited greater actual-prime overlap after instruction tuning. However, relative to pretrained variants, instruction-tuned outputs overlapped more with unrelated primes, showed a smaller actual-versus-random increment, and had lower conditional rule-reuse odds once target rule-set size was held constant. In exploratory analyses, each model exhibited greater mean lexical and semantic similarity to the preceding turn than the matched human responses did. Instruction-tuned models additionally produced responses with greater mean semantic similarity than their pretrained counterparts in all eight architecture pairs, whereas the lexical similarity results were more heterogeneous.

</details>


### [128] [UniMem: Complementary Episodic-to-Parametric Memory for Boundary-Agnostic Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26017)
*Siyu Xia,Chenheng Zhang,Yanting Wu,Haoxuan Li,Jiajun Chai,Xiaohan Wang,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Zhouchen Lin,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniMem提出了一种结合检索式记忆和参数化记忆的自路由框架，通过学习路由令牌自主管理记忆，在无需任务标签的情况下平衡稳定性与可塑性，实验表明在流式长序列任务中平均提升4.0个EM点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在边界模糊且不断演化的任务流中面临稳定性-可塑性困境：基于外部检索的记忆能快速吸收新信息但难以内化重复模式且带来推理开销，参数化记忆虽稳定高效但依赖显式任务边界和固定参数预算。受人类大脑记忆机制的启发，研究者希望实现一种能自主平衡灵活学习和稳定执行的记忆管理框架。

Method: UniMem采用可学习的路由令牌作为记忆控制器，协调两条互补记忆通路：1）情景缓冲器（检索式）用于保留新任务或稀疏任务；2）可扩展的参数化记忆用于巩固重复出现的可靠模式。通过将任务识别与任务执行解耦，利用路由令牌和参数化记忆块实现按需扩展，无需任务标签即可自主控制参数增长。

Result: 在长时流式任务序列的实验结果表明，UniMem在三种骨干模型上一致优于所有基线方法，同时保持执行忠实度，平均获得4.0个Exact Match分数提升。

Conclusion: UniMem有效地解决了LLM智能体记忆管理中的稳定性-可塑性权衡问题，通过自路由机制实现了无需任务标签的按需记忆扩展，为构建能够在真实动态环境中持续学习的智能体提供了可行的记忆架构方案。

Abstract: Memory is essential for LLM agents to accumulate task experience and reuse task-specific execution strategies. However, real-world deployment over boundary-agnostic and evolving task streams exposes a fundamental stability-plasticity dilemma. External retrieval-based memory can rapidly absorb new evidence, but it often fails to internalize recurring execution patterns and incurs inference-time retrieval overhead. Parametric memory enables stable and efficient execution once learned, but typically relies on explicit task boundaries and fixed parameter budgets. Inspired by the human brain, which balances plasticity and stability through complementary episodic storage and gradual consolidation, we propose UniMem, a self-routing framework for autonomous memory management. UniMem uses learnable routing tokens as memory controllers, enabling adaptive coordination between complementary memory pathways: novel or sparse tasks are retained in an episodic buffer for retrieval-augmented execution, while recurring and reliable patterns are consolidated into expandable parametric memory. By decoupling task identification from task execution with routing tokens and parametric memory blocks, UniMem expands memory on demand without task labels during deployment or uncontrolled parameter growth. Experiments on long-horizon streaming task sequences show that UniMem consistently outperforms baselines while maintaining execution fidelity, achieving an average gain of 4.0 EM points across three backbone models.

</details>


### [129] [Pass the Baton: Trajectory-Relayed On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26057)
*Haolei Xu,Xiaowen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Zixuan Ni,Hongxing Li,Yiwen Qiu,Weiming Lu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Relay-OPD方法，通过检测学生模型在错误推理前缀上的失败点，让教师模型短暂接管生成正确轨迹，随后学生继续生成并优化，有效解决了on-policy distillation中的前缀失败问题，在数学推理任务上显著提升性能并减少训练轨迹长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation（OPD）存在前缀失败问题：学生一旦走错推理方向，后续生成都建立在错误基础上，导致监督信号不可靠且浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出Relay-OPD方法，利用教师-学生在失败前缀上的连续性差异作为检测触发点，让教师短暂接管生成教师段，之后学生继续生成完整轨迹并据此优化。通过有限的中继预算将干预集中在关键早期位置。

Result: 在8个数学推理基准上，使用Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507教师和Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking学生，Relay-OPD在每个基准上都取得最佳或次佳结果，1.7B模型平均比标准OPD高5.73%，比最强基线FastOPD高1.49%，训练轨迹长度减少超过50%。

Conclusion: Relay-OPD通过有选择性地在关键位置引入教师干预，有效解决了前缀失败问题，显著提升了蒸馏效率和模型性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) grounds token-level supervision in the student's own trajectory, yet suffers from prefix failure: once the student commits to a wrong reasoning direction, all subsequent generation builds on this deviation, producing misdirected continuations that elicit unreliable supervision and waste compute. We identify a teacher-student continuation asymmetry on failed prefixes, where the teacher tends to redirect while the student continues along the original direction, and convert it into a label-free handoff trigger in Relay On-Policy Distillation (Relay-OPD). During training, Relay-OPD constructs relay trajectories by letting the teacher briefly take over at detected trigger points to produce a teacher leg, after which the student resumes and is optimized on the resulting trajectory. A limited relay budget concentrates intervention on critical early positions while limiting departure from the student policy. With a Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 teacher and Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking students on eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, Relay-OPD achieves the best or second-best results on every benchmark, outperforming standard OPD by +5.73% and the strongest baseline FastOPD by +1.49% on average for 1.7B, with consistent gains at 0.6B. Training trajectory length is reduced by over 50%.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Fourier Feature Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Elasto-Plastic Analysis of Geomaterials with a Non-Associative Mohr-Coulomb Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25150)
*Apisit Robjanghvad,Sompote Youwai*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种傅里叶特征物理信息神经网络（FF-PINN），用于解决岩土工程中非关联Mohr-Coulomb弹塑性问题。通过随机傅里叶特征映射缓解谱偏差，结合多目标损失函数和应变自适应采样策略，FF-PINN在位移和应力预测上分别实现了约66%和27%的误差降低，训练时间减半，精度和效率均优于传统PINN和FEM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元法（FEM）求解弹塑性边值问题计算成本高，而物理信息神经网络（PINN）虽无网格化但存在谱偏差，难以捕捉弹塑性边界和局部化塑性区的尖锐梯度，尤其对于非关联Mohr-Coulomb模型（压力相关屈服面、剪胀流动法则导致塑性区更窄、应力梯度更陡）这一问题更为突出。

Method: 在输入层嵌入随机傅里叶特征映射以缓解谱偏差；设计多目标损失函数（同时施加平衡方程、本构关系和KKT条件）；采用应变自适应采样策略提高关键区域分辨率。

Result: 在三个基准测试中，FF-PINN在大多数预测场中取得了更优精度：位移误差降低约66%，应力分量误差降低约27%；塑性破坏区几何形状更接近FEM结果；训练轮次减半，训练时间缩短一半；在训练数据量、配点密度、损失权重和2.0%噪声水平下均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FF-PINN为岩土工程弹塑性分析提供了一种计算高效且物理一致的可替代FEM的框架。

Abstract: Elasto-plastic boundary value problems in geotechnical engineering are conventionally solved by the Finite Element Method (FEM), which incurs high computational cost from incremental-iterative procedures. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) offer a mesh-free alternative but suffer from spectral bias, failing to resolve the sharp gradients arising at elastic-plastic boundaries and within localized plastic zones. This limitation is particularly consequential for the non-associative Mohr-Coulomb model, whose pressure-dependent yield surface and dilatant flow rule generate narrower plastic zones and steeper stress gradients than pressure-independent criteria. This study proposes a Fourier Feature Physics-Informed Neural Network (FF-PINN) for two-dimensional elasto-plastic problems governed by this model. Random Fourier feature mapping is embedded into the input layer to mitigate spectral bias, supported by a multi-objective loss function enforcing equilibrium, constitutive relations, and Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions against high-fidelity FEM data, together with a strain-adaptive sampling strategy. Benchmarked across three test cases, FF-PINN achieves superior accuracy across most predicted fields, with error reductions up to approximately 66 percent in displacement and 27 percent in stress components, and reproduces the plastic failure zone geometry with markedly closer fidelity to FEM. Sensitivity analysis confirms robustness across training data size, collocation density, loss weighting, and noise levels up to 2.0 percent. FF-PINN converges in half the training epochs required by the conventional PINN, halving wall-clock training time while achieving greater predictive accuracy. The framework therefore offers a computationally efficient and physics-consistent alternative to FEM for elasto-plastic geotechnical analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Beyond "What to Retrieve": Uncertainty in Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24884)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Xiaoli Lian,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpenCoder是一个不确定性感知框架，通过估计不同证据来源的不确定性来过滤、排序和引导仓库级代码生成，在RepExec-inline评估中将GPT的准确率从56.25%提升至78.13%，但提升效果具有后端依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强方法主要优化检索相关性，但未显式建模检索证据中的不确定性（噪声、冗余、冲突信号）对下游代码生成的影响。

Method: 提出OpenCoder框架：1) 估计不同来源（API知识、仓库上下文、相似代码）的不确定性；2) 基于不确定性过滤和排序异质证据；3) 引导生成、验证和修复过程。

Result: 在32任务的RepExec-inline上，GPT selected-output正确率从56.25%提升至78.13%；但与验证-修复对照组相当；Gemini上的提升无统计显著性；因子分析发现不存在通用的最优来源排序，跨来源交互显著。

Conclusion: 不确定性可作为仓库级代码检索、验证和修复的可操作控制信号，但效果依赖于LLM后端。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation relies on heterogeneous evidence whose relevance, compatibility, and completeness are inherently uncertain. Similar-code examples, repository context, and project-specific APIs may provide complementary information, but can also introduce noisy, redundant, or conflicting signals. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches primarily optimize retrieval relevance without explicitly modeling how uncertainty in retrieved evidence affects downstream generation. We introduce OpenCoder, an uncertainty-aware framework that estimates source-specific uncertainty, uses it to filter and rank heterogeneous evidence, and guides generation, verification, and repair. A factorial analysis over API knowledge, repository context, and similar-code evidence reveals no universal additive source ranking; instead, significant cross-source interactions depend on the accompanying evidence and LLM backend. On an expanded 32-task RepoExec-inline evaluation, OpenCoder improves GPT selected-output correctness over Baseline RAG from 56.25\% to 78.13\%. However, it matches a verification-and-repair control, and the corresponding Gemini improvement is not statistically supported, indicating backend-dependent benefits. Target-aware API refinement also substantially improves API-set retrieval. These findings support treating uncertainty as an actionable control signal for repository-level retrieval, verification, and repair.

</details>


### [132] [Characterizing Structural Testability in JavaScript: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24965)
*Shahrzad Mirzaei,Saba Alimadadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对JavaScript系统的结构性可测试性进行了大规模实证研究，提出了包含七个维度的可测试性框架和Composite Testability Score (CTS)，分析了30个开源项目，发现结构上难以测试的函数集中在少数文件中，且存在多种重复的结构配置模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然软件可测试性在面向对象和并发软件中已有大量研究，但针对现代JavaScript系统的结构性可测试性研究相对匮乏。JavaScript依赖异步执行、事件驱动控制流、闭包和动态交互等特性，现有可测试性框架无法充分捕捉这些特征。

Method: 将结构性可测试性操作化为七个维度：可控性、可观测性、分支复杂度、异步协调、事件驱动行为、封装性和副作用强度。通过AST静态分析提取这些维度，并聚合为Composite Testability Score (CTS)，应用于30个不同领域和规模的开源JavaScript项目。

Result: 结构性挑战高的函数集中在少数文件中，这些函数通过多种重复的结构配置模式产生，而非单一主导模式。研究还揭示了项目特征与可测试性之间的关联。

Conclusion: 该研究为JavaScript结构性可测试性提供了新的见解，为未来在测试工作量估算、自动化测试生成、可测试性感知的重构以及软件质量评估等方面的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Software testability has long been recognized as a software quality attribute that influences testing effort and effectiveness. While prior work has extensively studied testability in object-oriented and concurrent software, comparatively little is known about structural testability in modern JavaScript systems. JavaScript applications rely on asynchronous execution, event-driven control flow, closures, and dynamic interactions that are not explicitly captured by existing testability frameworks. This paper presents a large-scale empirical study of structural testability in JavaScript. We operationalize structural testability as a seven-dimensional construct capturing controllability, observability, branching complexity, asynchronous coordination, event-driven behaviour, encapsulation, and side-effect intensity. These dimensions are derived from AST-based static analysis and aggregated into a Composite Testability Score (CTS) for comparative analysis across functions, files, and projects. We apply this framework to 30 open-source JavaScript projects spanning diverse domains and sizes. Our analysis characterizes the distribution of structural testability, identifies recurring structural archetypes among low-CTS functions, and examines associations between project characteristics and testability. We find that structurally-challenging functions are concentrated within a relatively small subset of files and arise through multiple recurring structural configurations rather than a single dominant pattern. These findings provide new insight into structural testability in JavaScript and establish a foundation for future research on testing effort, automated test generation, testability-aware refactoring, and software quality assessment.

</details>


### [133] [Preliminary Guidelines for Using and Evaluating GenAI Tools to Support Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24991)
*Barbara Kitchenham,Sebastián Pizard,Lech Madeyski,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Martin Shepperd,David Budgen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对在系统文献综述（SLR）中使用生成式AI（GenAI）和大语言模型（LLM）的问题，提出了一套名为GUEST的过程建议，强调GenAI需要人类监督，目前无法独立完成系统性研究，但可在重复性任务和部分复杂任务验证中提供辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GenAI和LLM越来越多地被用于学术任务（包括SLR），但它们能否满足SLR所需的严谨性、可靠性和透明度尚无保证。为支持研究者合理使用或评估GenAI辅助SLR，需要明确的指导原则。

Method: 1. 进行快速综述，识别已有关于在SLR中使用和评估GenAI/LLM的指南研究；2. 结合思想实验、相关文献指导以及自身经验，提出使用和评估GenAI的建议。

Result: 识别了评估GenAI用于SLR时面临的问题；阐释了在规划、执行和报告使用GenAI的SLR及其评估时需考虑的过程问题；最终总结为一套名为GUEST的过程建议。

Conclusion: GenAI需要人类监督，目前不具备无监督进行系统性研究的能力，但可为部分重复性任务和复杂任务验证提供经济有效的辅助。GUEST建议有助于软件工程研究者开展并报告可信的、使用GenAI的SLR，并提供严谨的独立评估研究。

Abstract: Context: Generative AI (GenAI) and Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for academic tasks in software engineering and beyond, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). However, while capable of summarizing text, there is no guarantee they can meet the rigour, reliability, and transparency that SLRs require. Objectives: To support researchers intending to conduct SLRs using GenAI or those conducting empirical studies evaluating how well GenAI supports SLR tasks. Methods: First, we conducted a rapid review to identify studies that propose guidelines for evaluating and using GenAI and LLMs to support SLRs. Second, we drew on thought experiments, relevant guidance from the literature, and our own experience conducting SLRs and evaluating tools to develop recommendations for how to use and assess GenAI in the context of SLRs. Results: We discuss the problems researchers face when evaluating GenAI for SLRs. We identify and explain process issues to consider when planning, conducting, and reporting both SLRs using GenAI and evaluations of GenAI tools. Finally, we summarize our results as a set of process recommendations, which we name GUEST (GenAI Use and Evaluation in SLR Tasks). Conclusion: We argue that GenAI requires human oversight and is not currently capable of unsupervised systematic studies. However, it offers the prospect of cost-effective assistance for some repetitive tasks and for additional validation of some complex tasks. Our GUEST recommendations should help software engineering researchers both to conduct and report trustworthy SLRs using GenAI and to provide rigorous independent evaluation studies.

</details>


### [134] [Authoring Agent Skills: A Software-Engineering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25032)
*Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent技能应遵循软件工程原则（单一职责、接口与实现分离、低耦合、共享Token预算经济性），并以Claude Code为参考实现，详细说明了技能的结构、分阶段加载机制、描述编写方法，以及与其他行为控制机制（项目记忆文件、斜杠命令、子代理、外部工具连接、钩子）的对比选择规则，最后讨论了基于评估的创作流程、常见模式与缺陷，以及第三方技能的信任问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理中可复用过程性知识（即Agent技能）的构建缺乏系统方法论，作者希望将软件工程原则引入技能创作，以提升技能的质量、可维护性和可组合性。

Method: 以Claude Code为参考实现，定义了技能的结构化格式和分阶段加载机制；提出基于软件工程原则（单一职责、接口/实现分离、低耦合、Token预算经济性）的设计准则；建立选择不同行为控制机制的决策规则（基于谁决定运行以及提供何种保证）；提出评估驱动的创作流程。

Result: 给出了技能与其他机制（项目记忆文件、斜杠命令、子代理、外部工具、钩子）的对比定位图（UML类图风格），展示了技能在会话中的加载时机和作用点；总结了常见创作模式、典型缺陷以及第三方技能信任问题。

Conclusion: Agent技能本质上是软件制品，应遵循软件工程原则。通过评估驱动的创作流程和清晰的机制选择规则，可以更有效地构建和使用技能，但第三方技能的信任问题仍需进一步解决。

Abstract: Agent Skills are an emerging way to extend large language model agents with reusable procedural knowledge that the agent loads on demand. Anthropic introduced Agent Skills and published the format as an open specification supported across several agent tools. This note argues that a skill is a software artefact and that its construction should follow software-engineering principles, with qualifications: single responsibility, separation of interface from implementation, low coupling, and economy in a shared token budget, together with behavioural evaluation in place of deterministic testing. Using Claude Code as the reference implementation, it describes how a skill is structured, how its contents are loaded in stages, and how to write the description on which selection depends. It places skills against the other mechanisms a developer can use to shape agent behaviour, like project memory files, slash commands, subagents, external tool connections, and hooks, and gives a rule for choosing between them based on who decides that a mechanism runs and what guarantee it provides. It then sets out an evaluation-driven authoring process, a set of patterns and faults commonly encountered in authoring, and the trust question raised by using skills from third parties. We illustrate the comparison drawn in UML class style, the loading model, the anatomy of a skill, the relative position of each mechanism, and the points at which skills and hooks act during a session.

</details>


### [135] [What Motivates Whom? A Survey of Newcomers to OSS and Experienced OSS Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25126)
*Shashiwadana Nirmani,Hourieh Khalajzadeh,Mojtaba Shahin,Xiao Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过208名从业者的问卷调查，发现人口统计因素（年龄、性别、OSS角色）与贡献者动机显著相关，且他们对项目特征（项目年龄、开发阶段、文档质量）的偏好取决于具体动机。新手与经验丰富的OSS从业者在项目选择偏好上存在差异。研究还探讨了从业者对改进推荐系统的看法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件从业者在选择OSS项目时常遇到困难，导致入职效率低下和参与度下降。理解贡献者如何选择OSS项目对于支持贡献者入职、参与和长期留存至关重要。现有研究对新老贡献者的差异化需求缺乏深入比较。

Method: 通过对208名从业者进行在线问卷调查，收集人口统计信息、动机、项目选择偏好以及对推荐系统的看法等数据，采用比较视角分别分析新人和经验丰富从业者的回答。

Result: 年龄、性别、OSS角色等人口因素与贡献者动机显著相关；项目年龄、开发阶段、文档质量等特征的偏好因具体动机而异；新人与有经验的OSS从业者在项目选择偏好上存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果为研究者、OSS项目拥有者和软件从业者提供了改善贡献者入职、参与和留存的见解，同时为未来项目推荐系统的设计提供了参考，有助于改善OSS生态系统。

Abstract: Open source software (OSS) development continues to expand, yet software practitioners often struggle to select suitable projects, leading to inefficient onboarding and disengagement. Understanding how contributors select OSS projects is important for supporting contributors onboarding, engagement, and long-term participation within OSS communities. This study investigates contributors' project-selection preferences in OSS projects and examines how these preferences correlate with contributors' motivations and demographic backgrounds. Through an online survey of 208 practitioners, we found that demographic factors, such as age, gender, and the OSS role they held, significantly correlate with their motivations. Additionally, preferences for project characteristics such as project age, development stage, and documentation quality vary based on specific motivations. Importantly, our findings are presented through a comparative lens, analyzing the responses of newcomers to OSS and experienced OSS practitioners separately to uncover their distinct preferences. Lastly, we explore software practitioners' perspectives on how existing recommendation systems could better support project selection and align with their motivations. By disentangling the unique needs of newcomers to OSS and OSS practitioners, our findings provide insights for researchers, OSS project owners, and software practitioners to improve contributor onboarding, engagement, and retention, while also informing future project recommendation systems and improving the OSS ecosystem.

</details>


### [136] [Learning from 53.6K Real-World Developer Edits of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25130)
*Jenny T. Liang,Mihika Bairathi,Wayne Chi,Ameet Talwalkar,Nishant Subramani,Valerie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DECODE数据集，包含53.6K个人工智能生成代码的真实IDE编辑记录，并展示了其在分析开发者编辑行为和训练代码编辑预测模型方面的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM主要使用Git提交数据训练，但Git数据只包含最终成功的代码，缺乏细粒度的编辑过程信息。高质量、真实的代码编辑数据匮乏，需要构建更细致的编辑数据集。

Method: 收集了来自1000+开发者的53.6K条Python、TypeScript和JavaScript的IDE内代码编辑数据，构建DECODE数据集，并进行数据分析和模型评测。

Result: 1) 大多数编辑发生在接受AI补全后的15分钟内，31%的编辑轨迹会移除AI生成的内容；2) 在DECODE上微调的3B开源模型在代码编辑预测任务上显著优于前沿LLM。

Conclusion: 强调了未来AI编程助手需要采用以开发者为中心的机器学习方法，DECODE数据集为此提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Imperfections in AI-generated code require that software developers modify the generated code manually, or by re-prompting an AI programming assistant. Manual code edits provide more realistic and granular information on editing behavior than Git commits, which only contain final successful code snippets. Yet, due to a lack of high-quality, realistic code editing data, LLMs are mostly trained on publicly available Git data (e.g., commits). To address this gap, we introduce DECODE (Developer Edits of Code Dataset), a dataset of 53.6K real-world in-IDE code edits of AI-generated code in Python, TypeScript, and JavaScript, sourced from 1K+ developers. First, we demonstrate the utility of DECODE for data analysis, obtaining insights on when, why, and how AI-generated code is edited. We find that most edits occur within the first 15 minutes after accepting an AI completion, resulting in the removal of AI completions in 31% of edit trajectories. Second, we use DECODE to benchmark the ability of LLMs to predict code edits. We find that finetuning on DECODE enables open-source 3B models to perform code edit prediction tasks significantly better than frontier LLMs. We then discuss implications of this work, emphasizing the necessity of developer-centric machine learning approaches for future AI programming assistants.

</details>


### [137] [Specification-Driven DevOps for Multi-Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25141)
*Oleg Grynets,Kyrylo Fursov,Vasyl Lyashkevych,Volodymyr Veres*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估前沿LLM从仓库内容生成Docker部署配置的能力，发现虽能实现功能可用，但在架构安全、生成环境意图等非功能维度存在系统性缺失，并形式化区分了功能正确性与部署意图保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的可执行环境未必符合架构、安全、工作流和生产意图，现有研究缺少对这种"功能可用但意图偏离"的评估与形式化分析。

Method: 选取三个异构仓库（Python/Node.js/.NET/React/Rust/Java/Redis/PostgreSQL/MySQL），使用前沿LLM生成Dockerfile和Compose配置，通过确定性端到端HTTP测试和人工结构对比进行评估。

Result: 三个环境均功能可用（仅一个需基础镜像版本更新）；模型正确重建了服务拓扑、端口、依赖、主机名、后台worker、代理配置和文件级secrets；但系统性地遗漏网络分段、多阶段构建、缓存、热重载、生产前端服务、端口限制和跨平台构建逻辑。

Conclusion: 形式化了功能正确性与部署意图保真度的区分，并推导出无法从仓库可靠推断的信息所需的最小显式部署规范。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate executable software environments from repository artifacts. However, functional executability does not necessarily imply conformity with architectural, security, workflow, and production intent. This study investigates whether a frontier LLM can generate Dockerfiles and Docker Compose configurations for multi-service applications using repository contents without access to developer-authored deployment artifacts. Three heterogeneous repositories combining Python, Node.js, .NET, React, Rust, Java, Redis, PostgreSQL, and MySQL-compatible infrastructure were evaluated using deterministic end-to-end HTTP oracles and manual structural comparison. All three generated environments became functionally operational, although one required a Rust base-image update from version 1.85 to 1.88. The model correctly reconstructed service topology, application ports, infrastructure dependencies, service hostnames, a background worker, hidden proxy configurations, and a file-based Docker secrets mechanism. However, it consistently omitted network segmentation, multi-stage builds, dependency-layer caching, live-reload volumes, production frontend serving, restrictive backend-port policies, and cross-platform build logic. Based on these observations, the study formalizes the distinction between functional correctness and deployment-intent fidelity and analytically derives a minimal explicit deployment specification for information that cannot be reliably inferred from repository artifacts.

</details>


### [138] [NFR-to-Code Traceability in a Blockchain-IoT System: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25325)
*Yifei Wang,Jacky Keung,Xiaoxue Ma,Shijie Zhang,Yishu Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了非功能性需求（NFR）到代码的可追溯性问题，基于真实区块链-IoT项目构建了人工标注数据集，发现NFR追溯比FR更困难，其中安全相关NFR最具挑战性，主要难点不在于需求本身，而在于实现证据在代码中分散分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求到代码的可追溯性研究主要集中在功能性需求（FR）上，非功能性需求（NFR）的追溯（如安全、可靠性等关键质量属性）更加困难且研究不足，这阻碍了关键质量问题的验证。

Method: 基于真实区块链-IoT项目，设计了跨异构工件的注释协议，构建了包含FR和NFR链接的人工标注子集，并评估了四种检索基线方法：TF-IDF、BM25、LSI和WMD。

Result: FR到代码的追溯始终比NFR到代码的追溯更容易；安全相关NFR是最难追溯的子集；NFR追溯的主要挑战不在于需求可用性，而在于实现证据在代码中分散且不明确局部化。

Conclusion: NFR到代码的可追溯性研究具有独特挑战，未来需要针对NFR特性设计专门的追溯方法，尤其是安全相关的NFR。

Abstract: Requirement-to-Code traceability has been widely studied, yet existing research and public benchmarks remain largely centered on functional requirements (FRs). In contrast, traceability for non-functional requirements (NFRs) remains more difficult and underexplored, which hinders the verification of critical quality concerns such as security and reliability.This paper studies NFR-to-Code traceability based on a real-world blockchain-IoT project. We design an annotation protocol for constructing trace links across heterogeneous artifacts and build a manually curated subset containing both FR and NFR links. Using this subset, we examine the characteristics of NFR traceability and further evaluate four representative retrieval baselines: TF-IDF, BM25, LSI, and WMD. The results show that FR-to-Code tracing is consistently easier than NFR-to-Code tracing, while security-related NFRs are the most difficult subset. They further indicate that the main challenge of NFR traceability lies not in requirement availability, but in implementation evidence that is distributed and not clearly localized in code.

</details>


### [139] [Specula: Scaling formal specifications for autonomous model checking of system code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25333)
*Qian Cheng,Saad Mohammad Rafid Pial,Ruize Tang,Yiming Su,Emilie Ma,Finn Hackett,Ivan Beschastnikh,Yu Huang,Tianyin Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Specula 是一个全自动的智能体系统，利用 LLM 编码智能体自动生成 TLA+ 形式规约，并通过自演化循环持续改进规约质量，用于对大型系统代码进行模型检测和漏洞发现。在 48 个开源项目中发现了 249 个 bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统形式化方法依赖人类专家编写规约，门槛极高；而纯 LLM 驱动的技术存在奖励作弊（reward hacking）和幻觉等问题。作者希望消除形式化方法应用于实际系统代码的障碍。

Method: 采用基于 LLM 的编码智能体自主生成 TLA+ 规约（包括不变量和形式模型），并通过自演化循环（self-evolving loops）让智能体逐步加深对系统代码及其行为的理解，迭代提升规约质量。

Result: 在 48 个开源系统项目中检测到 249 个 bug，其中包括许多现有方法难以发现的深层 bug。已被多家公司使用。

Conclusion: Specula 实现了全自动的形式化验证流程，有效结合 LLM 的灵活性和形式化方法的严谨性，大幅降低了形式化方法的应用门槛，并在实际系统中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Specula is a push-button agentic system that generates high-quality formal specifications for large, complex system code and uses the specifications for highly effective model checking and bug finding. Specula employs large language model (LLM) based coding agents to autonomously develop TLA+ specifications, including invariants that describe correctness properties of the target system and formal models that describe the system implementation with the right level of abstractions. Specula is fully autonomous and thus eliminates the barrier of applying formal methods to real-world system code (as in traditional human-centric approaches). Meanwhile, Specula addresses limitations of LLM-driven techniques like reward hacking and hallucinations through self-evolving loops that iteratively improve specification quality by enabling the agents to deepen their understanding of system code and its behaviors. We have used Specula to check 48 open-source system projects; Specula found 249 bugs including many deep bugs that are hard to find by existing approaches. Specula has been used by several companies and is maintained at https://github.com/specula-org/Specula.

</details>


### [140] [CodeNib: A Multi-View Data System for Serving Repository Context to Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25431)
*Zhongming Yu,Hengjia Yu,Boqin Yuan,Shuting Zhao,Yizhao Chen,Aryan Dokania,Mihir Jagtap,Jiayu Chang,Yitong Ma,Yash Jayswal,Wentao Ni,Hejia Zhang,Zhaoling Chen,Gangda Deng,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeNib 是一个为编码智能体设计的多视图仓库上下文服务系统，通过在提交级别构建可复用的词法、稠密和结构视图，并维护跨编辑的选择性视图，实现了比独立重建快8.7倍（图更新）和25.4倍（向量更新）的性能，同时减少了50-87%的轨迹令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在演化中的代码仓库中反复搜索、导航和保留上下文，但现有的断开索引、语言服务器和任务本地历史记录迫使重复发现，且生命周期成本不透明。

Method: CodeNib 为每个仓库提交构建可复用的词法、稠密和结构视图，将输出映射到仓库相对源范围，跨编辑维护选定视图，并通过单一运行时提供排名搜索、符号导航和有界上下文。

Result: 在100个快照上，图更新和向量更新中位数比独立重建分别快8.7倍和25.4倍；在静态导航子集（1000个请求中的63%）上，每次请求的实时/静态延迟中位数比率为4.7倍；跨五个模型，选定上下文策略比配对grep/read减少了50-87%的轨迹令牌。

Conclusion: 支持多视图仓库上下文服务，并具有明确的操作特异性有效性边界。

Abstract: Coding agents repeatedly search, navigate, and retain context from evolving repositories, but disconnected indexes, language servers, and task-local histories force repeated discovery and obscure lifecycle costs. CodeNib builds reusable lexical, dense, and structural views per repository commit, maps outputs to repository-relative source ranges, maintains selected views across edits, and serves ranked search, symbol navigation, and bounded context through one runtime.
  Across 100 snapshots, we map quality-cost frontiers across the repository-context lifecycle. When outputs match an independent rebuild, graph and vector updates are $8.7\times$ and $25.4\times$ faster at the median. On the static-navigation subset matching normalized live-server locations (63% of 1,000 requests), the median per-request live/static latency ratio is $4.7\times$. Across five models, selected context policies preserve localization with 50--87% fewer trajectory tokens than paired grep/read. Together, these results support multi-view repository-context serving with explicit, operation-specific validity boundaries.

</details>


### [141] [Repositories, Contributors, and Continuity: An Empirical Study of Foundational Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25437)
*Vincent Gierisch,Nicole Hoess,Ralf Ramsauer,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文运用实证软件工程方法分析量子软件仓库，通过跨仓库活动和贡献者关系研究社区演化，发现贡献者迁移模式和跨项目知识转移证据，并观察到项目可能演变为持续社区、融入成熟生态系统或通过新软件制品延续活动等多种发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件生态系统快速发展，但许多仓库的开发投入有限，其概念是否能在个体仓库之外持续存在尚不明确，因此需要探究概念和影响力如何跨仓库边界延续。

Method: 应用成熟的实证软件工程技术，结合跨仓库活动与贡献者关系，分析一组基础量子软件仓库的社区演化。

Result: 提供了贡献者迁移模式和跨项目知识转移的实证证据，观察到多种发展路径。

Conclusion: 初步揭示了量子软件生态系统中概念和影响力如何在仓库边界之间持续存在的实证视角。

Abstract: Driven by contributions from academia, industry, and open-source communities, the quantum software ecosystem is rapidly growing. Across this ecosystem, new concepts often emerge through software artefacts accompanying scientific publications as well as through sustained development in larger communities. However, many repositories receive development efforts only over a limited period of time, raising the question whether their concepts persist beyond individual repositories. In this paper, we apply established empirical software engineering techniques to analyse a set of foundational quantum software repositories. We combine cross-repository activity with contributor relationships to study the evolution of communities. Our analysis provides empirical evidence of contributor migration patterns and indications of cross-project knowledge transfer. We observe multiple development paths: projects may evolve into sustained communities, contributors may integrate concepts into established ecosystems, or activity may continue through new and follow-up software artefacts. Our observations provide an initial empirical perspective on how concepts and influence persist across repository boundaries in quantum software ecosystems.

</details>


### [142] [Input Relation Prompting for Metamorphic Testing on Query-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25603)
*Eng-Shen Tu,Shin-Jie Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种辅助测试者为查询系统（QBS）识别蜕变关系（MR）的方法，通过提示输入输出关系来缓解预言问题，无需真实数据和预定义测试用例，可组合其他测试方法，并通过真实Web应用案例验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询系统（QBS）缺乏测试的真实数据（ground truth），手动测试耗时耗力，传统方法依赖领域知识且效率低下。

Method: 通过提示蜕变关系（MR）来辅助测试者描述输入输出间的预期关系，利用蜕变测试（MT）在不依赖真实数据的情况下检测故障，并可结合组合测试和模糊测试。

Result: 通过一个真实Web应用的案例研究，验证了该方法的适用性和潜在价值。

Conclusion: 该方法推进了蜕变测试领域，为QBS测试者提供了提升测试效率的有效工具。

Abstract: Testing query-based systems (QBSs) presents significant challenges due to the absence of ground truth for validation and the extensive time and effort required for manual testing. This paper addresses these challenges by proposing an approach that assists testers in identifying metamorphic relations (MRs) for metamorphic testing (MT) instead of solely and exhaustively relying on prerequisite domain knowledge. MT is an approach rising in popularity employed to alleviate the oracle problem by applying input transformation rules (MRs) to a program. The proposed approach helps the tester by prompting MRs that describe the relationships between inputs and outputs, enabling fault detection when the expected relationship is not met. Unlike traditional testing approaches, this approach does not rely on pre-defined test cases or concrete ground truth, making it suitable for the testing of real-world QBSs. Furthermore, the proposed approach can be combined with other testing methods such as combinatorial testing and fuzz testing, expanding the possibilities for QBS testing. A conducted case study of a real-world web application demonstrates the applicability and potential of the proposed approach. Overall, this research contributes to advancing the field of metamorphic testing and provides a valuable tool for QBS testers to enhance their testing efficiency.

</details>


### [143] [SkillGate: Cost Efficient Runtime Malicious Skill File Detection in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25619)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillGate，一个针对AI编码代理技能包的安全网关系统，通过混合正则预过滤+LLM判断管道检测恶意技能文件，在SkillsBench基准上实现F1=0.817、FPR=1.13%，并将LLM输入token减少77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理（如Cursor、Claude Code、GitHub Copilot）使用的技能包文件存在严重供应链安全风险——恶意技能文件可重编程代理行为、窃取凭证、注入后门或重定向到攻击者控制的端点，而目前缺乏系统化的防御工具链。

Method: SkillGate采用混合管道：先通过正则表达式预过滤快速识别安全信号文件（跳过LLM判断），对可疑文件仅将匹配的片段窗口发送给LLM评判器，而非完整内容，从而大幅减少LLM输入token量。

Result: 在SkillsBench基准（n=1,650，9.1%为恶意）上，SkillGate达到F1=0.817、FPR=1.13%，LLM输入token减少77%，AUPRC（0.830）优于现有工具5-6倍（0.144/0.162）。

Conclusion: SkillGate是首个系统化的AI编码代理技能包安全防御方案，在检测效果、成本效率和运行时开销方面均表现优异，有效填补了这一新型供应链攻击面的防御空白。

Abstract: Software engineering teams now deploy AI coding agents (Cursor, Claude Code, GitHub Copilot) as first-class productivity tools, installing domain-specific skill files to tailor agent behavior to project APIs, framework conventions, and organizational workflows. These complex Markdown files are easily downloaded from public registries with a single npx skills add command and no real security screening, representing a novel supply-chain attack surface: a malicious skill file can silently reprogram agent behavior, exfiltrating credentials, injecting backdoors into generated code, or redirecting agent actions to attacker-controlled endpoints. The threat is not hypothetical: recent reports document hundreds of malicious skill packages in public registries, including organized campaigns that distributed credential-stealing infostealers via fake productivity skills. No systematic toolchain defense exists for this attack surface. We present SkillGate, a deployable security gateway that screens AI skill packages before coding agent installation. SkillGate uses a hybrid regex-prefilter + LLM-judge pipeline: safe-signal files bypass the LLM entirely (skip savings); flagged files have only their matched snippet windows sent to the judge, not the full content (snippet savings). We answer four research questions covering detection effectiveness, screening cost, runtime overhead, and false positive behavior on the SkillsBench benchmark against two existing tools. On SkillsBench (n=1,650, 9.1% malicious), SkillGate achieves F1=0.817, FPR=1.13% while reducing LLM input tokens by 77% vs. full-file screening, and outperforming existing tools by 5-6x on threshold-independent AUPRC (0.830 vs. 0.144/0.162).

</details>


### [144] [An Empirical Study of Model Context Protocol Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25635)
*Muhammad Hamza Arshad Majeed,May Mahmoud,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对GitHub上1,723个MCP应用进行大规模研究，分析模型上下文协议（MCP）生态系统中客户端应用的实践现状。研究发现：85.2%的应用使用配置文件管理服务器，81.1%使用官方SDK，但配置文件命名没有统一约定；在人类监督方面，90.8%有日志记录，77.2%支持启用/禁用控制，但仅37.2%实现了工具执行前的阻塞式审批步骤，导致大多数MCP应用中LLM可以无条件调用已启用的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议虽然标准化了LLM应用与外部工具的通信，但应用侧缺乏规范，开发者集成MCP服务器时没有配置、通信或人工监督的约定。现有研究集中在服务器端，对消费这些服务器的客户端应用研究不足。
